            lastSentJSON = '';
            sendUpdate();
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_MANUAL_TABULATION') {
            manualTabulationInProgress = true;
            let result;
            try {
                result = await openManualTabulationArea();
            } finally {
                setTimeout(() => { manualTabulationInProgress = false; }, 1200);
            }
            window.parent.postMessage({
                type: 'ROCKETCHAT_TAB_OPEN_RESULT',
                source: 'firewall-extension',
                ok: !!result.ok,
                message: result.message || ''
            }, '*');
            return;
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'SEND_AUTO_REPLY') {
            const result = await sendAutoReplyMessage(event.data.message || '');
            window.parent.postMessage({
                type: 'ROCKETCHAT_AUTO_REPLY_RESULT',
                source: 'firewall-extension',
                ok: !!result.ok,
                message: result.message || ''
            }, '*');
            return;
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_NPS_FORWARD') {
            const result = await openNpsForwardFlow();
            window.parent.postMessage({
                type: 'ROCKETCHAT_NPS_RESULT',
                source: 'firewall-extension',
                ok: !!result.ok,
                message: result.message || ''
            }, '*');
            return;
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_FILA_FORWARD') {
            const result = await openFilaForwardFlow();
            window.parent.postMessage({
                type: 'ROCKETCHAT_FILA_RESULT',
                source: 'firewall-extension',
                ok: !!result.ok,
                message: result.message || ''
            }, '*');
            return;
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_ABANDONO_FLOW') {
            const result = await openAbandonoFlow();
            window.parent.postMessage({
                type: 'ROCKETCHAT_ABANDONO_RESULT',
                source: 'firewall-extension',
                ok: !!result.ok,
                message: result.message || ''
            }, '*');
            return;
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_APPLY_TAGS') {
            const result = await applyTagsFromParent(event.data.tags || []);
            window.parent.postMessage({
                type: 'ROCKETCHAT_APPLY_TAGS_RESULT',
                source: 'firewall-extension',
                ok: !!result.ok,
                message: result.message || '',
                tags: result.tags || []
            }, '*');
            return;
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_FETCH_TAG_OPTIONS') {
            const result = await fetchTagOptionsFromParent();
            window.parent.postMessage({
                type: 'ROCKETCHAT_FETCH_TAG_OPTIONS_RESULT',
                source: 'firewall-extension',
                requestId: event.data.requestId || null,
                ok: !!result.ok,
                message: result.message || '',
                options: result.options || [],
                tags: result.tags || []
            }, '*');
            return;
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_CHAT_BY_NAME') {
            const chatName = (event.data.chatName || '').trim();
            const chatUrl = (event.data.chatUrl || '').trim();
            if (!chatName) return;
            let tries = 0;
            let navigatedByUrl = false;
            const targetNorm = normalizeText(chatName);
            const tryOpen = setInterval(() => {
                tries++;
                const titleElements = document.querySelectorAll(SIDEBAR_ITEM_TITLE_SELECTOR);
                for (let titleEl of titleElements) {
                    const currentName = (titleEl.textContent || '').trim();
                    const exact = currentName === chatName;
                    const tolerant = normalizeText(currentName) === targetNorm;
                    if (exact || tolerant) {
                        const linkEl = titleEl.closest('a[href]');
                        if (linkEl) {
                            linkEl.click();
                            clearInterval(tryOpen);
                            setTimeout(() => { lastSentJSON = ''; sendUpdate(); }, 400);
                            setTimeout(() => {
                                const closeBtn = document.querySelector('[data-qa="SidebarHeaderActionClose"]') || 
                                               document.querySelector('[data-qa="ContextualbarActionClose"]') ||
                                               document.querySelector('button[aria-label*="Close" i]');
                                if (closeBtn) {
                                    closeBtn.click();
                                }
                            }, 600);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (!navigatedByUrl && chatUrl && tries >= 8) {
                    navigatedByUrl = true;
                    try {
                        const abs = new URL(chatUrl, window.location.origin).href;
                        if (window.location.href !== abs) {
                            window.location.href = abs;
                        }
                    } catch (_) {}
                }

                if (tries >= 40) {
                    clearInterval(tryOpen);
                }
            }, 300);
        }

        if (event.data.type === 'TOGGLE_OMNICHANNEL') {
            // acao explicita do usuario: pode abrir o kebab se precisar (nao e polling passivo)
            toggleOmnichannelResilient(() => { lastSentJSON = ''; sendUpdate(); });
        }
    });

    // inicializacao

    window.parent.postMessage({
        type: 'ROCKETCHAT_BRIDGE_READY',
        source: 'firewall-extension',
        frameUrl: window.location.href,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    }, '*');

    // envio periodico
    setInterval(() => { lastSentJSON = ''; }, 30000);

    if (isMainFrame) {
        // frame main
        setInterval(() => {
            sendUpdate();
            autoDetectAndExtract();
        }, SCAN_INTERVAL);

        setTimeout(sendUpdate, 2000);

    } else if (isSlotFrame) {
        // frame slot
        setInterval(() => {
            autoDetectAndExtract();
            sendSlotSignal();
        }, SCAN_INTERVAL);

        setTimeout(sendSlotSignal, 900);

    } else {
        // frame outro
        setInterval(() => {
            sendUpdate();
            autoDetectAndExtract();
        }, SCAN_INTERVAL);

        setTimeout(sendUpdate, 2000);
    }

})();

    <script>
        // ================================================================
        // Constants
        // ================================================================
        const N2_DEPT = 'AgPki67wjCZpN5X2W';
        const QUEUE_AGENT_ID = '2p7YLxPWbNHSkurQ9'; // agente/bot que recebe os CRs em fila
        const MIN_DEPARTMENTS = 10;
        const TOTAL_MIN_DEPARTMENTS = 12; // usado sÃ³ no card "Total de Agentes" (inclui deslogados)
        const AGENT_PALETTE = [
            '#7c4dff', '#3d8bff', '#26a69a', '#ec407a', '#ab47bc',
            '#5c6bc0', '#42a5f5', '#26c6da', '#66bb6a', '#ffa726',
        ];
        // ================================================================
        // State
        // ================================================================
        const state = {
            apiUrl: 'https://helptechho.digitalaec.com.br',
            token: null,
            userId: null,
            me: null,
            userStatus: 'offline',
            queueCount: 'â€”',
            agents: [],
            roomsByAgent: new Map(),
            totalQualifiedAgents: 0,
            sortMode: 'default',
            chatsSortDir: 'desc',
            alphaSortDir: 'asc',
            updateIntervals: []
        };
        // ================================================================
        // Helpers
        // ================================================================
        function pickColor(str) {
            let hash = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
                hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + str.charCodeAt(i);
                hash |= 0;
            }
            return AGENT_PALETTE[Math.abs(hash) % AGENT_PALETTE.length];
        }
        function initials(name) {
            return name.split(' ').slice(0, 2).map(n => n[0]).join('').toUpperCase();
        }
        function isN2(agent) {
            return Array.isArray(agent.departments) && agent.departments.some(d => (d._id || d) === N2_DEPT);
        }
        function statusLabel(status) {
            const labels = {
                online: 'DisponÃ­vel', away: 'Ausente', away_almoco: 'Pausa AlmoÃ§o',
                away_feedback: 'Pausa Feedback', busy: 'Ocupado', offline: 'Deslogado'
            };
            return labels[status] || status;
        }
        function statusDotClass(status) {
            if (status === 'online' || status === 'available') return 'online';
            if (status === 'away' || status === 'away_almoco' || status === 'away_feedback') return 'away';
            if (status === 'busy') return 'busy';
            return 'offline';
        }
        // dot do card de operador reflete se ele ACEITA novos chats (statusLivechat), nÃ£o a presenÃ§a bruta
        function livechatDotClass(agent) {
            return agent.statusLivechat === 'available' ? 'online' : 'busy';
        }
        function agentRank(agent) {
            const av = agent.statusLivechat === 'available';
            const on = agent.status === 'online';
            const n2 = isN2(agent);
            const offlineAndUnavailable = agent.status === 'offline' && !av;
            if (offlineAndUnavailable) return 6;
            if (n2 && av && on) return 0;
            if (av && on) return 1;
            if (n2 && av) return 2;
            if (av) return 3;
            if (n2) return 4;
            return 5;
        }
        function sortAgents(agents) {
            const list = [...agents];
            switch (state.sortMode) {
                case 'chats': {
                    const dir = state.chatsSortDir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
                    list.sort((a, b) => {
                        const ca = state.roomsByAgent.get(a._id) || 0;
                        const cb = state.roomsByAgent.get(b._id) || 0;
                        return (ca - cb) * dir;
                    });
                    break;
                }
                case 'unavailable': {
                    list.sort((a, b) => {
                        const aAv = a.statusLivechat === 'available' ? 1 : 0;
                        const bAv = b.statusLivechat === 'available' ? 1 : 0;
                        if (aAv !== bAv) return aAv - bAv;
                        return (a.name || a.username).localeCompare(b.name || b.username, 'pt-BR');
                    });
                    break;
                }
                case 'alpha': {
                    const dir = state.alphaSortDir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
                    list.sort((a, b) => (a.name || a.username).localeCompare(b.name || b.username, 'pt-BR') * dir);
                    break;
                }
                default:
                    list.sort((a, b) => agentRank(a) - agentRank(b));
            }
            return list;
        }
        function updateSortButtonsUI() {
            const chatsBtn = document.getElementById('sort-chats-btn');
            const unavBtn = document.getElementById('sort-unavailable-btn');
            const alphaBtn = document.getElementById('sort-alpha-btn');
            chatsBtn.classList.toggle('active', state.sortMode === 'chats');
            unavBtn.classList.toggle('active', state.sortMode === 'unavailable');
            alphaBtn.classList.toggle('active', state.sortMode === 'alpha');
            chatsBtn.querySelector('.sort-arrow').textContent = state.sortMode === 'chats' ? (state.chatsSortDir === 'asc' ? 'â†‘' : 'â†“') : 'â†•';
            alphaBtn.querySelector('.sort-arrow').textContent = state.sortMode === 'alpha' ? (state.alphaSortDir === 'asc' ? 'Aâ†’Z' : 'Zâ†’A') : 'â†•';
        }
        async function sha256hex(text) {
            const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(text));
            return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
        }
        // ================================================================
        // API
        // ================================================================
        async function performLogin(apiUrl, username, digest) {
            const response = await fetch(apiUrl + '/api/v1/login', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ user: username, password: { digest, algorithm: 'sha-256' } })
            });
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('UsuÃ¡rio ou senha invÃ¡lidos');
            const data = await response.json();
            return { token: data.data.authToken, userId: data.data.userId };
        }
        async function login(username, password) {
            const digest = await sha256hex(password);
            const { token, userId } = await performLogin(state.apiUrl, username, digest);
            state.token = token;
            state.userId = userId;
            // Guardamos o digest (nÃ£o a senha em texto puro) para poder refazer o login
            // sozinho quando o token (validade de 1 dia) expirar.
            localStorage.setItem('rc_token', token);
            localStorage.setItem('rc_userId', userId);
            localStorage.setItem('rc_apiUrl', state.apiUrl);
            localStorage.setItem('rc_username', username);
            localStorage.setItem('rc_pwdigest', digest);
        }
        // Reautentica usando as credenciais salvas. Retorna true/false.
        async function reAuthenticate() {
            const username = localStorage.getItem('rc_username');
            const digest = localStorage.getItem('rc_pwdigest');
            const apiUrl = localStorage.getItem('rc_apiUrl') || state.apiUrl;
            if (!username || !digest) return false;
            try {
                const { token, userId } = await performLogin(apiUrl, username, digest);
                state.token = token;
                state.userId = userId;
                state.apiUrl = apiUrl;
                localStorage.setItem('rc_token', token);
                localStorage.setItem('rc_userId', userId);
                return true;
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Falha ao reautenticar automaticamente:', error);
                return false;
            }
        }
        async function apiGet(path, allowRetry = true) {
            try {
                const response = await fetch(state.apiUrl + path, {
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-Auth-Token': state.token || '',
                        'X-User-Id': state.userId || ''
                    }
                });
                if (response.status === 401) {
                    if (allowRetry) {
                        const ok = await reAuthenticate();
                        if (ok) return apiGet(path, false);
                    }
                    logout();
                    return null;
                }
                return await response.json();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('API Error:', error);
                return null;
            }
        }
        // ================================================================
        // Data Loading
        // ================================================================
        async function loadMe() {
            const data = await apiGet('/api/v1/me');
            if (data) {
                state.me = data;
                state.userStatus = data.status || 'offline';
                updateUserUI();
            }
        }
        async function loadQueueCount() {
            try {
                const d = await apiGet(`/api/v1/livechat/queue?agentId=${QUEUE_AGENT_ID}`);
                state.queueCount = d?.queue?.[0]?.chats ?? 'â€”';
                document.getElementById('queue-count').textContent = state.queueCount;
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar fila:', error);
                state.queueCount = 'â€”';
                document.getElementById('queue-count').textContent = 'â€”';
            }
        }
        async function loadRoomsByAgent() {
            try {
                let allEntries = [];
                let offset = 0;
                const pageSize = 100;
                let total = Infinity;
                while (offset < total) {
                    const d = await apiGet(`/api/v1/livechat/queue?count=${pageSize}&offset=${offset}&includeOfflineAgents=true`);
                    if (!d || !d.queue) break;
                    allEntries = allEntries.concat(d.queue);
                    total = d.total ?? allEntries.length;
                    offset += pageSize;
                    if (d.queue.length === 0) break;
                }
                const counts = new Map();
                allEntries.forEach(entry => {
                    const agentId = entry.user?._id;
                    const chats = entry.chats || 0;
                    if (agentId) counts.set(agentId, (counts.get(agentId) || 0) + chats);
                });
                state.roomsByAgent = counts;
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar salas por agente:', error);
                state.roomsByAgent = new Map();
            }
        }
        async function loadAgents() {
            try {
                const data = await apiGet('/api/v1/livechat/users/agent?count=200&offset=0');
                if (!data || !data.users) throw new Error('Sem dados');
                await loadRoomsByAgent();
                state.totalQualifiedAgents = data.users.filter(u =>
                    Array.isArray(u.departments) && u.departments.length > TOTAL_MIN_DEPARTMENTS && u.username !== 'fila.chat'
                ).length;
                const filtered = data.users.filter(u =>
                    Array.isArray(u.departments) && u.departments.length > MIN_DEPARTMENTS &&
                    !(u.status === 'offline' && u.statusLivechat !== 'available') && u.username !== 'fila.chat'
                );
                state.agents = sortAgents(filtered);
                renderAgents();
                updateStats();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar agentes:', error);
                document.getElementById('agents-list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-state-icon">âš ï¸</div><div>Erro ao carregar agentes</div></div>';
            }
        }
        // ================================================================
        // UI Updates
        // ================================================================
        function updateUserUI() {
            if (!state.me) return;
            const name = state.me.name || state.me.username;
            document.getElementById('user-avatar').textContent = initials(name);
            document.getElementById('user-name').textContent = name;
            document.getElementById('user-status').textContent = statusLabel(state.userStatus);
		console.log(statusDotClass(state.userStatus))
            document.getElementById('status-dot').className = 'dot ' + statusDotClass(state.userStatus);
        }
        function updateStats() {
            document.getElementById('total-agents').textContent = state.totalQualifiedAgents;
            document.getElementById('available-agents').textContent = state.agents.filter(a => a.statusLivechat === 'available').length;
            document.getElementById('online-agents').textContent = state.agents.filter(a => a.status === 'online').length;
        }
        function renderAgents() {

                return;
            }

                const bg = pickColor(agent.username);
                const label = statusLabel(agent.status || 'offline');

                const balloonLabel = agent.statusLivechat === 'available' ? 'BalÃ£o aberto' : 'BalÃ£o fechado';

const chatIcon = agent.statusLivechat === 'available'
    ? `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"

    : `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.5" class="solar solar-chat-round-off-linear chat-icon disabled"><path d="M4.92915 4.92969C3.1195 6.73933 2.00021 9.23933 2.00021 12.0008C2.00021 13.6004 2.37583 15.1124 3.04367 16.4532C3.22115 16.8095 3.28022 17.2168 3.17733 17.6014L2.58172 19.8274C2.32317 20.7937 3.20723 21.6778 4.17356 21.4192L6.3996 20.8236C6.78415 20.7207 7.19142 20.7798 7.54774 20.9573C8.88858 21.6251 10.4005 22.0008 12.0002 22.0008C14.7616 22.0008 17.2616 20.8815 19.0713 19.0718" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round"/>

<path d="M2 2C2.97631 2.97631 3.95262 3.95262 4.92893 4.92893C5.21199 5.21199 5.49505 5.49505 5.77811 5.77811C7.06458 7.06458 8.35106 8.35106 9.63753 9.63753C12.782 12.782 15.9266 15.9266 19.0711 19.0711C20.0474 20.0474 21.0237 21.0237 22 22" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>`;

                const deptCount = (agent.departments || []).length;

        function showLoginPage() {
            document.getElementById('login-section').style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('dashboard-section').classList.add('hidden');
        }
        async function showDashboard() {
            document.getElementById('login-section').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('dashboard-section').classList.remove('hidden');
            state.updateIntervals.forEach(id => clearInterval(id));
            state.updateIntervals = [];
            await loadMe();
            await loadQueueCount();
            await loadAgents();
            const interval = setInterval(async () => {
                await loadQueueCount();
                await loadAgents();
            }, 10000);
            state.updateIntervals.push(interval);
        }
        function logout() {
            state.token = null;
            state.userId = null;
            state.me = null;
            state.queueCount = 'â€”';
            state.updateIntervals.forEach(id => clearInterval(id));
            state.updateIntervals = [];
            localStorage.removeItem('rc_token');
            localStorage.removeItem('rc_userId');
            localStorage.removeItem('rc_apiUrl');
            localStorage.removeItem('rc_username');
            localStorage.removeItem('rc_pwdigest');
            showLoginPage();
        }

        document.getElementById('login-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const apiUrl = document.getElementById('api-url').value.trim();
            const username = document.getElementById('username').value.trim();
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const errorDiv = document.getElementById('login-error');
            const btn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
            if (!apiUrl || !username || !password) {
                errorDiv.textContent = 'Preencha todos os campos';
                errorDiv.classList.add('show');
                return;
            }
            try {
                state.apiUrl = apiUrl;
                btn.disabled = true;
                btn.textContent = 'Entrando...';
                errorDiv.classList.remove('show');
                await login(username, password);
                showDashboard();
            } catch (error) {
                errorDiv.textContent = error.message || 'Erro ao fazer login';
                errorDiv.classList.add('show');
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.textContent = 'Entrar';
            }
        });
        document.getElementById('password').addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') document.getElementById('login-btn').click();
        });
        document.getElementById('logout-btn').addEventListener('click', logout);
        document.getElementById('sort-chats-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (state.sortMode === 'chats') {
                state.chatsSortDir = state.chatsSortDir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
            } else {
                state.sortMode = 'chats';
                state.chatsSortDir = 'desc';
            }
            state.agents = sortAgents(state.agents);

            renderAgents();
        });
        document.getElementById('sort-unavailable-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            state.sortMode = state.sortMode === 'unavailable' ? 'default' : 'unavailable';
            state.agents = sortAgents(state.agents);
            updateSortButtonsUI();
            renderAgents();
        });
        document.getElementById('sort-alpha-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (state.sortMode === 'alpha') {
                state.alphaSortDir = state.alphaSortDir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
            } else {
                state.sortMode = 'alpha';
                state.alphaSortDir = 'asc';
            }
            state.agents = sortAgents(state.agents);
            updateSortButtonsUI();
            renderAgents();
        });
        // ================================================================
        // Init
        // ================================================================
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const savedToken = localStorage.getItem('rc_token');
            const savedUserId = localStorage.getItem('rc_userId');
            const savedApiUrl = localStorage.getItem('rc_apiUrl');
            if (savedToken && savedUserId && savedApiUrl) {
                state.token = savedToken;
                state.userId = savedUserId;

async function testAgentQueue(agentId) {
    const path = `/api/v1/livechat/queue?agentId=${encodeURIComponent(agentId)}`;

    try {
        const response = await fetch(state.apiUrl + path, {
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-Auth-Token': state.token || '',
                'X-User-Id': state.userId || ''
            }
        });

        const body = await response.text();

        console.log('Status HTTP:', response.status);
        console.log('Resposta bruta:', body);

        try {
            const data = JSON.parse(body);
            console.log('Resposta JSON:', data);
            return data;
        } catch {
            console.warn('A resposta nÃ£o Ã© um JSON vÃ¡lido.');
            return body;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro na requisiÃ§Ã£o:', error);
        return null;
    }
};

const IS_FILE_PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol === 'file:';
const ROCKET_HOME_URL = 'https://helptechho.digitalaec.com.br/home';

function canEmbedInIframe(url) {
    if (!url) return false;
    try {
        const parsed = new URL(url, window.location.href);
        return ['http:', 'https:'].includes(parsed.protocol);
    } catch (_) {
        return false;
    }
}

// Extrai ID do room a partir de uma URL do Rocket.Chat (para detectar troca de sessÃ£o)
function extractRoomIdFromUrl(url) {
    if (!url) return '';
    try {
        const u = new URL(url, window.location.origin);
        const path = u.pathname;
        let m = path.match(/\/omnichannel\/current\/([^\/?#]+)/i);
        if (m && m[1] && m[1] !== 'current') return m[1];
        m = path.match(/\/(?:channel|group|direct|room)\/([^\/?#]+)/i);
        if (m && m[1]) return m[1];
        return u.searchParams.get('rid') || u.searchParams.get('roomId') || '';
    } catch (_) {
        return '';
    }
}

function frameCanReceiveBridgeMessage(frameEl) {
    if (!frameEl || !frameEl.contentWindow) return false;
    const src = (frameEl.getAttribute('src') || '').trim();
    if (!src || src === 'about:blank') return false;
    return canEmbedInIframe(src);
}

async function carregarFluxosJSON() {
    if (IS_FILE_PROTOCOL) {
        
        return;
    }
    try {
        const response = await fetch('fluxos.json');
        if (response.ok) {
            guiaFluxos = await response.json();
            localStorage.setItem('guia_fluxos', JSON.stringify(guiaFluxos));
        }
    } catch (e) {
    }
}

function loadPendingAutoReplyMessages() {
    try {
        const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PENDING_AUTO_REPLY_CACHE_KEY) || '[]');
        const cleaned = Array.isArray(raw)
            ? raw.map(v => (v || '').toString().trim()).filter(Boolean)
            : [];
        slotAutoReplyMessages = cleaned.length ? cleaned : [...DEFAULT_PENDING_AUTO_REPLY_MESSAGES];
    } catch (_) {
        slotAutoReplyMessages = [...DEFAULT_PENDING_AUTO_REPLY_MESSAGES];
    }
}

function persistPendingAutoReplyMessages() {
    const cleaned = (slotAutoReplyMessages || [])
        .map(v => (v || '').toString().trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
    slotAutoReplyMessages = cleaned.length ? cleaned : [...DEFAULT_PENDING_AUTO_REPLY_MESSAGES];
    localStorage.setItem(PENDING_AUTO_REPLY_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(slotAutoReplyMessages));
}

async function waitForBridgeActivation(timeoutMs = 4500) {
    const startedAt = Date.now();

    ensureRocketBackgroundOnline(false);
    requestSidebarUpdate();

    while ((Date.now() - startedAt) < timeoutMs) {
        if (bridgeActive) return true;
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 220));
        requestSidebarUpdate();
    }

    return !!bridgeActive;
}

function loadOpenedAtCache() {
    try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CHAT_OPENED_AT_CACHE_KEY) || '{}');
        chatOpenedAtCache = parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
    } catch (_) {
        chatOpenedAtCache = {};
    }
}

function saveOpenedAtCache() {
    try {
        localStorage.setItem(CHAT_OPENED_AT_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(chatOpenedAtCache));
    } catch (_) {}
}

function setOpenedAtCache(chatName, openedAt) {
    const key = normalizeChatName(chatName || '');
    if (!key || !openedAt) return;
    chatOpenedAtCache[key] = {
        openedAt,
        touchedAt: Date.now()
    };
    saveOpenedAtCache();
}

function getDateKeyFromTimestamp(ts) {
    if (!ts) return '';
    const d = new Date(ts);
    if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
}

function getOpenedAtCache(chatName) {
    const key = normalizeChatName(chatName || '');
    if (!key) return null;
    const item = chatOpenedAtCache[key];
    if (!item || !item.openedAt) return null;
    if (!item.touchedAt || (Date.now() - item.touchedAt) > CHAT_OPENED_AT_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
        delete chatOpenedAtCache[key];
        saveOpenedAtCache();
        return null;
    }
    if (getDateKeyFromTimestamp(item.openedAt) !== getToday()) {
        delete chatOpenedAtCache[key];
        saveOpenedAtCache();
        return null;
    }
    return item.openedAt;
}

function removeOpenedAtCache(chatName) {
    const key = normalizeChatName(chatName || '');
    if (!key) return;
    if (chatOpenedAtCache[key]) {
        delete chatOpenedAtCache[key];
        saveOpenedAtCache();
    }
}

    const dot = document.getElementById('agent-status-dot');
    if (!dot) return;
    
    const isOnline = (status === 'online');
    agentOnline = isOnline;
    dot.className = 'agent-status-dot ' + (isOnline ? 'online' : 'offline');
}
